CORPORATE PROFILE                                                   公司簡介
SHOUGANG FUSHAN RESOURCES GROUP LIMITED                             首鋼福山資源集團有限公司
is one of the most sizable integrated coking coal corporations in   為中國中西部 最具規模之綜合焦煤企業之一。
CENTRAL-WESTERN CHINA.
Taking Shanxi Province as its major investment base, it             以山西省作為主要投資基地，
is principally engaged in MINING OF COKING COAL,                    主要從事 焦煤開採、原焦煤及精焦煤生產及銷
PRODUCTION AND SALES OF RAW AND CLEAN COKING
                                                                    售業務。
COAL.
The Group has three premium operating coking coal mines and         本集團現有三座在產的優質焦煤礦及三座洗煤廠。
three coal preparation plants.

OPERATING MINES                                                        營運中之煤礦

XINGWU COAL MINE                                                       興無煤礦

•   6 km south of Liulin County and the mining right area extended     •   位於柳林縣以南6公里，採礦權區佔地約
    over 11.6 sq. km and spans 4.5 km east to west and 4.5 km              11.6平方公里，東西相距4.5公里，南北相距
    north to south                                                         4.5公里

•   Operation commenced in 1968                                        •   於一九六八年開始營運

•   Annual approved raw coking coal production capacity: 1.75          •   核准年原焦煤生產能力：175萬噸
    million tonnes

•   Operate a coal preparation plant with annual designed input        •   經營一座年入洗量為120萬噸之洗煤廠（於二
    processing capacity of 1.2 million tonnes (put into operation in       零零二年十月投產）
    October 2002)

•   Mainly production of hard coking coal                              •   主要生產硬焦煤

OPERATING MINES (continued)                                             營運中之煤礦（續）

COAL CHARACTERISTICS                                                    煤炭特徵

•    Located within the Lishi-Liulin mining area of Hedong coalfield,   •      位於河東煤田之離柳礦區，該區為中國優質
     one of China’s key reserve areas for high-quality hard coking             硬焦煤之主要儲量區域之一。
     coal.

•    Regarded as “panda coal” because of its scarcity and high          •      由於其稀有性和高經濟價值，被譽為「熊貓
     economic value.                                                           煤」。

•    The Group’s coking coal is of particularly good quality due to     •      本集團之焦煤發熱值高及焦結度強，加上灰
     its high calorific value and caking index but low ash and sulfur          份及硫份低，品質優越，使之非常適合用於
     content, characteristics that are highly desirable for coke and           生產焦炭及鋼鐵。
     steel making.

ANTI-CORRUPTION                                                             反貪污

The Group has placed high emphasis on professional conduct and              本集團非常重視僱員的職業操守和道德誠信。本
integrity of the employees. The “Staff Code of Conduct” and all             集團的《員工守則》、各項管理規定都明確清正廉
administrative rules have expressly specified the values, visions and       潔的價值理念和操作指引。本集團特別編製《預
guidelines for maintaining honesty and corresponding operation              防職務犯罪管理辦法》，宣揚反腐倡廉教育、強
guidelines. The Group has specially laid down the “Administrative           化權力監督制約、建設預防腐敗體系、發揮紀律
Measures in respect of Prevention of Occupational Crimes” to                約束作用。
advocate anti-corruption and honesty, strengthen scrutiny of and
restrictions on authority, build up anti-corruption system and
capitalise on the effect of disciplinary restraints.

enhanced the production safety standards. By strengthening the              項整治，強化全員安全培訓、技能比武，使安全

CHAIRMAN AND MANAGING DIRECTOR                                                  主席及董事總經理

The roles of chairman and managing director are separate and                    為加強主席與董事總經理的獨立性及問責性，彼
exercised by different individuals to reinforce their independence and          等的角色已予區分，並由不同人士擔任。丁汝才
accountability. Mr. Ding Rucai is the Chairman and Mr. Li Shaofeng              先生為主席，而李少峰先生則為本公司的董事總
was the managing director of the Company until Mr. Fan Wenli took               經理，直至范文利先生於二零一九年十一月一日
up the role on 1 November 2019. The Chairman provides leadership                接替該職務。主席的職責負責領導董事會，確保
for the Board and ensures that the Board works effectively and                  董事會有效地運作，且履行其職責；本公司董事
performs its responsibilities. The managing director of the Company             總經理則肩負行政總裁整體的職務，負責本集團
has overall chief executive responsibility for the Group’s business             整體的業務發展及日常管理。主席與本公司董事

The statement of the auditor of the Company, PricewaterhouseCoopers,         本公司的核數師羅兵咸永道會計師事務所已在本
about its reporting responsibilities on the audited consolidated financial   年報第116頁至第124頁的獨立核數師報告中就彼

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                               股息政策

The Board formalised and adopted a dividend policy for the Company            董事會為本公司正式制訂及採納於二零一八年十
which took effect from 12 December 2018 and it aims to allow the              二月十二日起生效之股息政策，旨在讓股東分享
Shareholders to participate in the Company’s profits whilst retaining         本公司溢利時，同時預留足夠儲備以供本集團日
adequate reserves for the future growth of the Group.                         後發展之用。

The Company intends to distribute not less than 40% of its net profit         本公司擬將每個財政年度不少於40%的股東應佔
attributable to the shareholders (not less than dividend payout ratio         純利（股息發放率不少於40%)分派為股息，惟董
of 40%) in each financial year as dividends. However, the Board will          事會於考慮宣派或建議派發股息時，將會考慮本
take into account certain factors of the Group when considering               集團多項因素，包括實際及預期財務表現、保留
the declaration or recommendation of dividends, which includes the            溢利及可供分派儲備、流動資金狀況、整體經濟
actual and expected financial performance, retained earnings and              狀況、本集團業務的營運週期、可能對本集團業
distributable reserves, liquidity position, general economic conditions,      務或財務表現及狀況造成影響的其他內外因素以
business cycle of the Group’s businesses and other internal or external       及董事會可能認為相關的其他因素。
factors that may have an impact on the businesses or financial
performance and position of the Group, and other factors that the
Board may consider relevant.

Subject to Shareholders’ approval of the proposed final dividend at           待股東於二零二零年股東週年大會上批准擬派發
the AGM 2020, the dividend payout out ratio for the year ended 31             的末期股息後，截止二零一九年十二月三十一日
December 2019 is approximately 80%.                                           止年度的全年股息發放率約為80%。

SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                                       購股權計劃

On 25 May 2012, the shareholders of the Company adopted a share           於二零一二年五月二十五日，本公司股東批准採
option scheme (the “Share Option Scheme”).                                納了一個購股權計劃（
                                                                                   「該購股權計劃」
                                                                                          ）。

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to enable the Company            該購股權計劃旨在使本公司能向選定參與者授出
to grant share options to selected participants as incentives or           購股權，作為激勵或獎勵彼等對本公司及╱或任
rewards for their contribution or potential contribution to the            何其附屬公司及╱或本集團任何成員公司持有任

 Change of relationship:           When the agreement was entered into, Shougang International was a
 關係轉變：                             substantial shareholder of the Company, its holding company, Shougang
                                   Group, was also a substantial shareholder of the Company. Accordingly,
                                   the transactions between the Group and Shougang Group and/or its
                                   associates became connected transactions for the Company under the
                                   Listing Rules. Since the Landlord was an indirect wholly-owned subsidiary
                                   of Shougang Group, the transaction under the Tenancy Agreement
                                   constituted a continuing connected transaction for the Company under
                                   the Listing Rules.
                                   簽訂協議時，由於首長國際為本公司之主要股東，其控股公司首鋼集團
                                   亦為本公司之主要股東，因此，根據上市規則，本集團與首鋼集團及╱
                                   或其聯繫人士之交易構成本公司之關連交易。鑒於業主為首鋼集團之間
                                   接全資附屬公司，根據上市規則，租賃協議項下之交易構成本公司之一
                                   項持續關連交易。

                                   On 9 April 2019, Shougang Group ceased to be a holding company of
                                   Shougang International, and thus the Landlord was no longer a connected
                                   person (as per the definition of the Listing Rules) of the Company. The
                                   transactions contemplated under this agreement would not constitute a
                                   connected transaction from this date onwards.
                                   於二零一九年四月九日，首鋼集團不再是首長國際之控股公司，故此業
                                   主不再屬本公司的關連人士（按照上市規則定義）。自該日起，本協議項
                                   下擬進行的交易不會構成關連交易。

ANNUAL REVIEW ON CONTINUING CONNECTED                                      持續關連交易之年度審閱
TRANSACTIONS

Pursuant to Rule 14A.56 of the Listing Rules, the Company’s auditor,       根據上市規則第14A.56條，本公司之核數師羅兵
PricewaterhouseCoopers, was engaged to report on the Group’s               咸永道會計師事務所根據香港會計師公會頒佈之
continuing connected transactions in accordance with Hong Kong             《香港鑒證業務準則》第3000號（經修訂）
                                                                                               「非審核
Standard on Assurance Engagements 3000 (Revised) “Assurance                或審閱過往財務資料之鑒證工作」，並參照《實務
Engagements Other Than Audits or Reviews of Historical Financial           說明》第740號「關於香港上市規則所述持續關連
Information” and with reference to Practice Note 740 “Auditor’s            交易的核數師函件」就本集團之持續關連交易出
Letter on Continuing Connected Transactions under the Hong Kong            具報告。根據上市規則第14A.56條，羅兵咸永道
Listing Rules” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public       會計師事務所已發出無保留意見函件，其中包含
Accountants. PricewaterhouseCoopers has issued an unqualified              有關本集團於上文披露之持續關連交易結果及結
letter containing findings and conclusions in respect of the continuing    論。本公司已向聯交所提供核數師函件之副本。
connected transactions disclosed by the Group in the paragraph
above in accordance with Rule 14A.56 of the Listing Rules. A copy of
the auditor’s letter has been provided by the Company to the Stock
Exchange.

In accordance with Rule 14A.55 of the Listing Rules, the Independent       根據上市規則第14A.55條，獨立非執行董事已
Non-executive Directors reviewed the continuing connected                  審閱上述段落之持續關連交易，並確認該等交易

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether            我們的目標是合理確定此等綜合財務報表整體是

     2.2 Changes in accounting policies                                       2.2 會計政策變動

          This note explains the impact of the adoption of HKFRS 16                本附註解釋採納香港財務報告準則第16
          on the Group’s financial statements.                                     號對本集團財務報表之影響。

          Adoption of HKFRS 16                                                     採納香港財務報告準則第16號

          The Group has adopted HKFRS 16 retrospectively from 1                    本集團自二零一九年一月一日起追溯採
          January 2019, but has not restated comparatives for the                  納香港財務報告準則第16號，惟按該準
          2018 reporting period, as permitted under the specific                   則之特定過渡條文所允許，未重列二零
          transitional provisions in the standard. The reclassifications           一八年報告期間之比較數字。因此，因
          and the adjustments arising from the new leasing rules are               採用新租賃規則而作出之重新分類及調
          therefore recognised in the opening balances on 1 January                整於二零一九年一月一日期初結餘內確
          2019.                                                                    認。

          On adoption of HKFRS 16, the Group recognised lease                      採納香港財務報告準則第16號時，本集
          liabilities in relation to leases which had previously been              團就先前根據香港會計準則第17號「租
          classified as “operating leases” under the principles of                 賃」之原則分類為「經營租賃」之租賃確
          HKAS 17 Leases. These liabilities were measured at the                   認租賃負債。該等負債以剩餘租賃款項
          present value of the remaining lease payments, discounted                之現值計量，並使用於二零一九年一月
          using the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate as of 1                    一日之承租人增量借款利率貼現。於
          January 2019. The weighted average lessee’s incremental                  二零一九年一月一日，應用至租賃負
          borrowing rate applied to the lease liabilities on 1 January             債之加權平均承租人增量借款利率為
          2019 was 4.52%.                                                          4.52%。

                the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the             控制該實體。附屬公司於控制權轉

     2.4 Business combination                                                2.4 業務合併

          The acquisition method of accounting is used to account              本集團採用收購會計法將所有業務合併
          for all business combinations, regardless of whether equity          入賬，而不論是否已收購權益工具或其
          instruments or other assets are acquired. The consideration          他資產。收購一間附屬公司轉讓之代價
          transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary comprises            包括：
          the:

          •    fair values of the assets transferred;                          •   所轉讓資產之公平值；

          •    liabilities incurred to the former owners of the                •   被收購業務前擁有人所產生之負
               acquired business;                                                  債；

          •    equity interests issued by the Group;                           •   本集團已發行股本權益；

          •    fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a           •   或然代價安排產生之任何資產或負
               contingent consideration arrangement; and                           債之公平值；及

          •    fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the           •   附屬公司任何先前存在之股本權益
               subsidiary.                                                         之公平值。

          Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent          在業務合併中所收購之可識別資產以及
          liabilities assumed in a business combination are, with              所承擔之負債及或有負債，首先以其於
          limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair values          收購日期之公平值計量（少數例外情況
          at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-               除外）。本集團以逐項收購基準，按公

          entity’s net identifiable assets.

          over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired              超出所收購可識別資產淨值之公平值

          the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the business            於所收購業務之可識別資產淨值之公平
          acquired, the difference is recognised directly in profit or             值，則差額將直接於損益中確認為議價
          loss as a bargain purchase.                                              購買。

          Where settlement of any part of cash consideration is                    倘任何部分現金代價之結算獲遞延，日
          deferred, the amounts payable in the future are discounted               後應付金額貼現至其於兌換日期之現
          to their present value as at the date of exchange. The                   值。所用之貼現率乃該實體之增量借款
          discount rate used is the entity’s incremental borrowing                 利率，即根據相若之條款及條件可從獨
          rate, being the rate at which a similar borrowing could be               立金融機構獲得同類借款之利率。或然
          obtained from an independent financier under comparable                  代價分類為權益或財務負債。分類為財
          terms and conditions. Contingent consideration is classified             務負債之金額其後將重新按公平值計
          either as equity or a financial liability. Amounts classified            量，而公平值變動於損益中確認。
          as a financial liability are subsequently remeasured to fair
          value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss.

          value at the acquisition date. Any gains or losses arising               任何因該項重新計量產生之收益或虧損
          from such remeasurement are recognised in profit or loss.                於損益中確認。

     2.24 Provisions                                                            2.24 撥備

          to settle the obligation and the amount can be reliably                  會確認撥備，惟不會就日後之經營虧損
          estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future                      確認任何撥備。
          operating losses.

          over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic                負債餘額產生固定之定期利率。使用權

          each period. The right-of-use assets is depreciated over the              較短者為準）按直線法計提折舊。
          shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on a

          measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include              基準計量。租賃負債計及固定款項之現
          the net present value of the fixed payments (including                    值淨額（包括實質固定款項）減任何應收

          receivable.

          implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be determined, the             該利率未能釐定，則使用承租人之增量
          lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate               借款利率，即在類似經濟環境以及類似
          that the lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds                     條款及條件下就獲得價值相近資產承租
          necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in a similar                人為借入所需資金而將需支付之利率。
          economic environment with similar terms and conditions.

          To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                 為確定增量借款利率，本集團：

               since third party financing was received;                              來融資條件的變化；

               the Group, which does not have recent third party                      集團所持租賃的信貸風險進行了調

               country, currency and security.                                        限、國家、貨幣和擔保。

          The Group is exposed to potential future increases in                   本集團面臨基於指數或費率的未來可變
          variable lease payments based on an index or rate, which                租賃付款的潛在增加，直到生效後才
          are not included in the lease liability until they take effect.         包括在租賃負債中。基於指數或費率的
          When adjustments to lease payments based on an index or                 租賃付款調整生效時，將對租賃負債進
          rate take effect, the lease liability is reassessed and adjusted        行重新評估，並根據使用權資產進行調
          against the right-of-use asset.                                         整。

          Lease payments are allocated between principal and                      租賃付款在本金和財務成本之間分配。

          over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic              使每個期間的負債餘額產生固定的定期

          (c)   Liquidity risk                                                  (c)   流動資金風險

                Liquidity risk relates to the risk that the Group will                流動資金風險與本集團無法履行其
                not be able to meet its obligations associated with its               財務負債相關責任之風險有關。本
                financial liabilities. The Group is exposed to liquidity              集團面對償付應付貿易賬項及其融
                risk in respect of settlement of trade payables and                   資責任以及其現金流量管理有關之
                its financing obligations, and also in respect of its                 流動資金風險。本集團之目標為將
                cash flow management. The Group’s objective is                        流動資產及資金承擔維持於合適水
                to maintain an appropriate level of liquid assets                     平，以應付其短期及長期流動資金
                and committed lines of funding to meet its liquidity                  需求。
                requirements in the short and long term.

                The Group manages its liquidity needs by carefully                    本集團於日常業務中謹慎監察潛在
                monitoring expected payments for potential                            投資及現金流出之預期付款，以管
                investments as well as cash-outflows due in day-to-                   理其流動資金需要。本集團按日監
                day business. Liquidity needs are monitored on a day-                 察其流動資金需要。另每月釐定為
                to-day basis. Long-term liquidity needs for a 360-day                 期360日之長期流動資金需要。
                lookout periods are identified on a monthly basis.

35. SHARE OPTION SCHEME (continued)                                        35. 購股權計劃（續）

     The 2012 Scheme were designed to reward and provide                     二零一二年計劃是專為回報及向指定類別之
     incentives to, and strengthen the Group’s business relationship         參與者提供獎勵以及加強本集團與彼等之間
     with the prescribed classes of participants, including but not          之業務關係而設。該等指定類別之參與者包
     limited to eligible employees and directors of any member of the        括但不限於對本集團發展作出貢獻之本集團
     Group, who contributed to the development of the Group.                 任何成員公司之合資格僱員及董事。

     Under the 2012 Scheme, the exercise price of the options is to          按二零一二年計劃，購股權之行使價由董事
     be determined by the directors and is at least the highest of (i)       釐定，不得低於(i)於授出日期在聯交所每日
     the closing price of the Shares as stated in the Stock Exchange’s       報價表所報之股份收市價；(ii)於緊接授出日
     daily quotations sheet on the date of grant; (ii) average               期前五個營業日在聯交所每日報價表所報之
     closing price of the Shares as stated in the Stock Exchange’s           股份平均收市價；及(iii)於授出日期之股份面
     daily quotations sheets for the five business days immediately          值三者之最高者。每名二零一二年計劃之承
     preceding the date of grant; and (iii) the nominal value of the         授人須就獲授購股權支付1港元之代價。購
     Shares on the date of grant. Each of the grantees of the 2012           股權一般可由授出日期起至董事釐定之日期
     Scheme is required to pay HK$1 as consideration for the grant           為止行使，但在任何情況下概不可超過授出
     of share options. An option may generally be exercised during           日期起計10年。
     the period commencing on the date of grant and expiring on
     the date as determined by the directors, but in any event not
     more than 10 years from the date of grant.

     expected to be continued to be implemented across the country.         實施，並預期將繼續實施。截至本報告日
     Up to the date of this report, the Group was not aware of any          期，本集團尚未發現因COVID-19爆發而對財
     material adverse effects on the financial statements as a result       務報表造成任何重大不利影響。鑑於世界衛
     of the COVID-19 outbreak. As the World Health Organisation             生組織將COVID-19定性為全球大流行，預期
     announced COVID-19 outbreak as a pandemic, an economic                 經濟將會放緩或衰退，進而影響焦煤的需求
     slowdown or recession is expected which will affect the demand         及巿場價格，對本集團未來業績可能將會造
     and market price of coking coal. All of these may have a               成負面影響。本集團將會密切關注COVID-19
     negative impact on the future results of the Group. The Group          疫情的發展，評估其對本集團財務狀況和經
     will pay close attention to the development of the COVID-19            營成果的影響，並將採取措施積極應對。
     outbreak and evaluate its impact on the financial position
     and operating results of the Group, and the Group shall take
